add_namespace = fcf_politics_event
country_event = {
	id = fcf_politics_event.0
	title = {
		text = fcf_politics_event.0.title.a
		trigger = { check_expr = { value = 650 subtract = abstain_vote divide = 2 less_than = agree_vote } } 
	}
	title = {
		text = fcf_politics_event.0.title.b
		trigger = { NOT = { check_expr = { value = 650 subtract = abstain_vote divide = 2 less_than = agree_vote } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = fcf_politics_event.0.desc.a
		trigger = { check_expr = { value = 650 subtract = abstain_vote divide = 2 less_than = agree_vote } } 
	}
	desc = {
		text = fcf_politics_event.0.desc.b
		trigger = { NOT = { check_expr = { value = 650 subtract = abstain_vote divide = 2 less_than = agree_vote } } }
	}
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	after = {
		set_variable = { bill_var = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = voting_for_bill
		clr_country_flag = voting_for_change_csp
		set_variable = { agree_vote = 0 }
		set_variable = { abstain_vote = 0 }
		set_variable = { total_vote = 650 }
		clr_country_flag = bill_pass_need_434

		clr_country_flag = PSF_abstain_vote
		clr_country_flag = POU_abstain_vote
		clr_country_flag = PCOP_abstain_vote
		clr_country_flag = PAPF_abstain_vote

		refresh_cgf_seat = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_event.0.a
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		trigger = { 
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = bill_pass_need_434 }
				check_variable = { agree_vote > 433 } 
			}
			else = { check_expr = { value = 650 subtract = abstain_vote divide = 2 less_than = agree_vote } } 
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = voting_for_change_csp }
			meta_effect = {
				text = { CSP_appoint_[CSPVAR]_effect = yes }
				CSPVAR = "[?csp_change_member_variable]"
			}
		}
		else = {
			meta_effect = {
				text = { bill_[BILLVAR]_pass_effect = yes }
				BILLVAR = "[?bill_var]"
			}
		}
	}	
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_event.0.b
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		trigger = { 
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = bill_pass_need_434 }
				check_variable = { agree_vote < 434 } 
			}
			else = { NOT = { check_expr = { value = 650 subtract = abstain_vote divide = 2 less_than = agree_vote } } }
		}
	}	
}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_politics_event.1
	title = fcf_politics_event.1.title
	desc = fcf_politics_event.1.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_event.1.a
        ai_chance = { base = 50 }
		hidden_effect = { FCF_supply_chain_issues_modifier_judge = yes }
	}
    option = {
		name = fcf_politics_event.1.b
        ai_chance = { base = 50 }
		hidden_effect = { 
			set_cosmetic_tag = FRA_rouge 
			FCF_supply_chain_issues_modifier_judge = yes
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_politics_event.2
	title = fcf_politics_event.2.title
	desc = fcf_politics_event.2.desc
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_CNS = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_PSF = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_POU = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_CGT = -4 }
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_PCOP = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_UA = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_URSRS = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_LJR = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_PAPF = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_ETC = 0 }
			cgf_seat_change = yes

			set_temp_variable = { agreec_CNS = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreec_PSF = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreec_POU = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreec_CGT = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreec_PCOP = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreec_UA = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreec_URSRS = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreec_LJR = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreec_PAPF = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreec_ETC = 0 }
			cgf_agree_change = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_event.2.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_happened_tooltip
			custom_effect_tooltip = effect_politics
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_CNS = 1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = cgf_seat_change_CNS_tt
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_PSF = 2 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = cgf_seat_change_PSF_tt
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_POU = -2 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = cgf_seat_change_POU_tt
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_CGT = -4 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = cgf_seat_change_CGT_tt
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_PCOP = 2 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = cgf_seat_change_PCOP_tt
			set_temp_variable = { seatc_UA = 1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = cgf_seat_change_UA_tt
			
			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			set_temp_variable = { agreec_CNS = 1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = cgf_agree_change_CNS_tt
			set_temp_variable = { agreec_PSF = 2 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = cgf_agree_change_PSF_tt
			set_temp_variable = { agreec_PCOP = 2 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = cgf_agree_change_PCOP_tt
			set_temp_variable = { agreec_UA = 1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = cgf_agree_change_UA_tt
		}
	}
}

			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_event.11 days = 9 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_event.12 days = 11 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_event.13 days = 13 }

			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_event.15 days = 17 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_event.16 days = 19 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_event.17 days = 21 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_event.18 days = 23 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_event.19 days = 25 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_event.20 days = 27 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_event.21 days = 29 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_event.7.a
        ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		add_stability = 0.05
		activate_decision = FCF_election
		set_country_flag = election_time_1936
		unlock_decision_tooltip = election_1936_CNS_cooperate_with_POU
		unlock_decision_tooltip = election_1936_CNS_cooperate_with_LJR
		unlock_decision_tooltip = election_1936_PSF_cooperate_with_CGT
		unlock_decision_tooltip = election_1936_PSF_cooperate_with_URSRS

		custom_effect_tooltip = unlock_in_politics
	}
}

	id = fcf_politics_event.25
	title = fcf_politics_event.25.title
	desc = fcf_politics_event.25.desc
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_event.25.a
        ai_chance = { base = 30 }
		set_temp_variable = { t = CGT_faction^2 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { t = 0.01 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { t = seat_CGT }
		round_variable = t
		set_temp_variable = { plust = t }
		set_temp_variable = { reducet = plust }
		multiply_temp_variable = { reducet = -1 }

		custom_effect_tooltip = effect_politics
		set_temp_variable = { seatc_CNS = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seatc_PSF = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seatc_POU = plust }
		set_temp_variable = { seatc_CGT = reducet }
		set_temp_variable = { seatc_PCOP = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seatc_UA = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seatc_URSRS = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seatc_LJR = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seatc_PAPF = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seatc_ETC = 0 }
		cgf_seat_change = yes

		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
		set_temp_variable = { party_var = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { factionc_1 = -15 }
		set_temp_variable = { factionc_2 = 15 }
		set_temp_variable = { factionc_3 = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { factionc_4 = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { factionc_5 = 0 }
		party_faction_change = yes

		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
		set_temp_variable = { party_var = 4 }
		set_temp_variable = { t = CGT_faction^2 }
		set_temp_variable = { factionc_1 = t }
		multiply_temp_variable = { t = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { factionc_2 = t }
		set_temp_variable = { factionc_3 = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { factionc_4 = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { factionc_5 = 0 }
		party_faction_change = yes
	}
}
